\t}\n97: }\n98: \n99: func (mm MainModel) Init() tea.Cmd {\n100: \treturn mm.Spinner.Tick\n101: }\n102: \n103: func (mm MainModel) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n104: \tvar cmd \t\t\t\tswitch mm.CurrentView.(type) {\n123: \t\t\t\tcase defaultview.DefaultModel:\n124: \t\t\t\t\tmm.State = DefaultView\n125: \t\t\t\tcase latest.LatestModel:\n126: \t\t\t\t\tmm.State = \t\tmm.Loading = true\n139: \t\tmm.ErrMsg = \"\"\n140: \t\t// Ensure initial load is page 1, use PerPage from the model (defaulted in NewLatestModel)\n141: \t\tcmds = append(cmds, pass key messages if not loading and not handled above\n252: \t\t\tvar currentViewCmd tea.Cmd\n253: \t\t\tmm.CurrentView, currentViewCmd = mm.CurrentView.Update(msg)\n254: \t\t\tcmds = append(cmds, currentViewCmd)\n255: \t\t}\n256: \t}\n257: \n258: \treturn mm, tea.Batch(cmds...)\n259: }\n260: \n261: func (mm MainModel) View() string {\n262: \t// var mainViewContent string // Removed as it was hopt.LatestView)\n\t\tis.Equal(m.ErrMsg, \"\")\n\t\tis.True(m.CurrentView != nil)\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"should set error message on LatestResponseMsg failure\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tis := errors.New(errMsgContent)}\n\t\tupdatedModel, _ = m.Update(msg)\n\t\tm = updatedModel.(hopt.MainModel)\n\n\t\tis.Equal(m.Loading, false)\n\t\tis.Equal(m.ErrMsg, errMsgContent)\n\t\tis.Equal(m.State, originalState) // Should remain in the state it was in before loading started\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"should transition to loading on StartLoadingCategoriesMsg\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tis := DefaultView\n\n\t\t// 1. Go to LatestView\n\t\tupdatedModel, _ := m.Update(defaultview.StartLoadingLatestMsg{})\n\t\tm = updatedModel.(hopt.MainModel)\n\t\tupdatedModel, _ = m.Update(commands.LatestResponseMsg{Products: &commands.Products{}, Width: 80, Height: 24})\n\t\tm = updatedModel.(hopt.MainModel)\n\n\t\tis.Equal(m.State, hopt.LatestView)\n\t\tis.Equal(len(m.PreviousViews), 1)\n\n\t\t// 2.
